 385:                     """,
 386:                     runId,
 387:                     requestedCandles1m, historical1m, replay1m, fresh.oneMinuteSignals(), fresh.oneMinuteBuys(),
 388:                     requestedCandles5m, historical5m, replay5m, fresh.fiveMinuteSignals(), fresh.fiveMinuteBuys(),
 389:                     requestedCandles1h, historical1h, replay1h, fresh.oneHourSignals(), fresh.oneHourBuys(),
 390:                     fresh.errors(), shadow.trades(), shadow.wins(), shadow.losses(), shadow.realizedPnl(), shadow.finalWallet(),
 391:                     authorityCorrections, oldHardReversals, correctedHardReversals,
 392:                     cadencePass, authorityPass, passed, notes
 393:             );
 394:             parityComparisonNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 395: 
 396:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 397:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 398:                     SET status=?, progress_percent=100, current_step=?, heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 399:                     WHERE id=?
 400:                     """, passed ? "PASSED" : "FAILED", passed ? "Regression checks passed" : "Regression checks failed", runId);
 401: 
 402:         } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 403:             // FIX-090: only mark ERROR after the replay pipeline has actually unwound to here.
 404:             // This makes ERROR a trustworthy signal that Delete Data may proceed once active_worker=false.
 405:             log.warn("FIX-090: regression test run {} stopped by user request", runId);
 406:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 407:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 408:                     SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
 409:                         error_message='Replay stopped manually by user. Test data may now be deleted.',
 410:                         failure_step='Manual stop', failure_exception=NULL, failure_root_cause=NULL,
 411:                         failure_stack_trace=NULL, heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 412:                     WHERE id=?
 413:                     """, runId);
 414:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 415:             log.error("Regression test run {} failed", runId, exception);
 416:             String failedStep = currentStep(runId);
 417:             String summary = errorDetail(exception, 1900);
 418:             Throwable root = rootCause(exception);
 419:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 420:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 421:                     SET status='ERROR', progress_percent=100, current_step='Regression test failed', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 422:                         error_message=?, failure_step=?, failure_exception=?, failure_root_cause=?,
 423:                         failure_stack_trace=?, started_at=COALESCE(started_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 424:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 425:                     WHERE id=?
 426:                     """,
 427:                     summary, failedStep, exception.getClass().getName(),
 428:                     rootCauseDetail(root, 1000), stackTrace(exception), runId);
 429:         } finally {
 430:             // FIX-11J: persist one timing snapshot after the existing Replay path has completed or
 431:             // unwound. Diagnostics persistence is deliberately best-effort: it must never replace
 432:             // the Replay's real PASSED/FAILED/ERROR outcome or swallow the original exception.
 433:             long replayTotalNs = System.nanoTime() - replayTotalStartedNs;
 434:             persistReplayTimingsSafely(runId, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs,
 435:                     buildReplayDatasetNs, generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs,
 436:                     parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs);
 437: 
 438:             // FIX-087: always release ownership, including ERROR paths, so a genuinely stopped
 439:             // replay remains recoverable later using the same run id and original replay window.
 440:             activeRunIds.remove(runId);
 441:             stopRequestedRunIds.remove(runId);
 442:         }
 443:     }
 444: 
 445:     private void persistReplayTimingsSafely(long runId, Long loadHistoricalDataNs,
 446:                                             Long verifyEventResolutionNs, Long buildReplayDatasetNs,
 447:                                             Long generateFreshSignalsNs, Long shadowExecutionNs,
 448:                                             Long parityComparisonNs, long replayTotalNs) {
 449:         try {
 450:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 451:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 452:                     SET timing_load_historical_ns=?, timing_verify_event_resolution_ns=?,
 453:                         timing_build_replay_dataset_ns=?, timing_generate_fresh_signals_ns=?,
 454:                         timing_shadow_execution_ns=?, timing_parity_comparison_ns=?, timing_total_ns=?
 455:                     WHERE id=?
 456:                     """, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs, buildReplayDatasetNs,
 457:                     generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs, parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs, runId);
 458:         } catch (Exception timingPersistenceFailure) {
 459:             log.error("FIX-11J: unable to persist Replay timing diagnostics for run {}",
 460:                     runId, timingPersistenceFailure);
 461:         }
 462:     }
 463: 
 464:     /**
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
 467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }
 508: 
 509:     private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 511:                 symbol, interval, start, end);
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }
 534: 
 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 637:                             INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 638:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 639:                                  generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
 640:                                  execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
 641:                                  effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
 642:                                  confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
 643:                                  trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 644:                             VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 651:                             generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
 652:                             generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),
 653:                             generated.getDetectedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getDetectedRegime().name(),
 654:                             generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),
 655:                             generated.getConfirmedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getConfirmedRegime().name(),
 656:                             generated.getRegimeCandidateCount(), generated.getEntryAuthority(),
 657:                             generated.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), generated.getTrendScore(),
 658:                             generated.getVolumeScore(), generated.getMomentumScore());
 659:                     } finally {
 660:                         profiler.recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 661:                     }
 662: 
 663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
 664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
 665:                     // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
 666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
 667:                     // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
 668:                     // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
 669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 670:                     Long sourceSignalId;
 671:                     try {
 672:                         sourceSignalId = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 673:                                 ? replayDataset.sourceSignalId(
 674:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 675:                                 : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 676:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 677:                                         .map(TradeSignal::getId).orElse(null);
 678:                     } finally {
 679:                         profiler.recordLineageLookup(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 680:                     }
 681:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 682:                     try {
 683:                         persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
 684:                     } finally {
 685:                         profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 686:                     }
 687:                 }
 688:             } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 689:                 throw stop;
 690:             } catch (Exception exception) {
 691:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 692:                     errors++;
 693:                     saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
 694:                 }
 695:                 log.warn("Regression fresh-signal generation failed: run={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candle={}",
 696:                         runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
 697:             }
 698: 
 699:             if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
 701:                 long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 702:                 try {
 703:                     updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),
 704:                             "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);
 705:                 } finally {
 706:                     profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
 707:                 }
 708:             }
 709:         }
 710: 
 711:         } finally {
 712:             // FIX-11M must never turn a valid Replay into ERROR if diagnostic summary logging fails.
 713:             try {
 714:                 analysisService.finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 715:             } catch (Exception profilingException) {
 716:                 log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_ERROR run={} message={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false",
 717:                         runId, profilingException.getMessage());
 718:             }
 719:             profiler.logSummary(log, timeline.size());
 720:         }
 721:         return new FreshReplayStats(oneMinuteSignals, oneMinuteBuys, fiveMinuteSignals, fiveMinuteBuys,
 722:                 oneHourSignals, oneHourBuys, buys, watches, sells, strongSells, errors, generatedSignals);
 723:     }
 724: 
 725:     private void saveGenerationError(long runId, String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, String error) {
 726:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 727:                 INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 728:                     (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 729:                      generated_at, latest_price, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 730:                 VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
 731:                 """, runId, symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 732:                 Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval))),
 733:                 candle.getClosePrice(), error);
 734:     }
 735: 
 736:     /**
 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }
 777: 
 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }
 794: 
 795:     private boolean isBuy(SignalDecision decision) {
 796:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 797:     }
 798: 
 799:     /**
 800:      * FIX-11L Replay-only profiler for the fresh-signal generation loop. It deliberately
 801:      * aggregates timings in memory and emits one summary log after the stage; there are no
 802:      * per-signal log writes and no Production/shared-analysis branches.
 803:      */
 804:     private static final class ReplayFreshSignalProfiler {
 805:         private final long runId;
 806:         private final ReplayDataSource dataSource;
 807:         private final long startedNs = System.nanoTime();
 808:         private long snapshotNs;
 809:         private long analysisNs;
 810:         private long analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs;
 811:         private long lineageLookupNs;
 812:         private long tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs;
 813:         private long progressUpdateNs;
 814:         private long snapshotCalls;
 815:         private long analysisCalls;
 816:         private long analysisTestSignalPersistenceCalls;
 817:         private long lineageLookupCalls;
 818:         private long tradeSignalTestPersistenceCalls;
 819:         private long progressUpdateCalls;
 820:         private long maxSnapshotNs;
 821:         private long maxAnalysisNs;
 822: 
 823:         private ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 824:             this.runId = runId;
 825:             this.dataSource = dataSource;
 826:         }
 827: 
 828:         private void recordSnapshot(long elapsedNs) {
 829:             snapshotNs += elapsedNs;
 830:             snapshotCalls++;
 831:             maxSnapshotNs = Math.max(maxSnapshotNs, elapsedNs);
 832:         }
 833: 
 834:         private void recordAnalysis(long elapsedNs) {
 835:             analysisNs += elapsedNs;
 836:             analysisCalls++;
 837:             maxAnalysisNs = Math.max(maxAnalysisNs, elapsedNs);
 838:         }
 839: 
 840:         private void recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(long elapsedNs) {
 841:             analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs += elapsedNs;
 842:             analysisTestSignalPersistenceCalls++;
 843:         }
 844: 
 845:         private void recordLineageLookup(long elapsedNs) {
 846:             lineageLookupNs += elapsedNs;
 847:             lineageLookupCalls++;
 848:         }
 849: 
 850:         private void recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(long elapsedNs) {
 851:             tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs += elapsedNs;
 852:             tradeSignalTestPersistenceCalls++;
 853:         }
 854: 
 855:         private void recordProgressUpdate(long elapsedNs) {
 856:             progressUpdateNs += elapsedNs;
 857:             progressUpdateCalls++;
 858:         }
 859: 
 860:         private void logSummary(org.slf4j.Logger logger, int timelineSize) {
 861:             long totalNs = System.nanoTime() - startedNs;
 862:             long measuredNs = snapshotNs + analysisNs + analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs
 863:                     + lineageLookupNs + tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs + progressUpdateNs;
 864:             long otherNs = Math.max(0L, totalNs - measuredNs);
 865:             logger.info(
 866:                     "FIX11L_REPLAY_SIGNAL_PROFILE run={} dataSource={} timeline={} totalMs={} "
 867:                             + "snapshotMs={} snapshotCalls={} snapshotAvgMs={} snapshotMaxMs={} "
 868:                             + "analysisMs={} analysisCalls={} analysisAvgMs={} analysisMaxMs={} "
 869:                             + "analysisTestSignalPersistMs={} analysisTestSignalPersistCalls={} "
 870:                             + "lineageMs={} lineageCalls={} tradeSignalTestPersistMs={} tradeSignalTestPersistCalls={} "
 871:                             + "progressMs={} progressCalls={} otherMs={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false",
 872:                     runId, dataSource, timelineSize, millis(totalNs),
 873:                     millis(snapshotNs), snapshotCalls, averageMillis(snapshotNs, snapshotCalls), millis(maxSnapshotNs),
 874:                     millis(analysisNs), analysisCalls, averageMillis(analysisNs, analysisCalls), millis(maxAnalysisNs),
 875:                     millis(analysisTestSignalPersistenceNs), analysisTestSignalPersistenceCalls,
 876:                     millis(lineageLookupNs), lineageLookupCalls, millis(tradeSignalTestPersistenceNs),
 877:                     tradeSignalTestPersistenceCalls, millis(progressUpdateNs), progressUpdateCalls, millis(otherNs));
 878:         }
 879: 
 880:         private static String millis(long ns) {
 881:             return String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", ns / 1_000_000.0d);
 882:         }
 883: 
 884:         private static String averageMillis(long ns, long calls) {
 885:             return calls == 0L ? "0.000"
 886:                     : String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", (ns / 1_000_000.0d) / calls);
 887:         }
 888:     }
 889: 
 890:     private record ReplayCandle(String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, boolean primary) {}
 891: 
 892:     private record FreshReplayStats(
 893:             int oneMinuteSignals, int oneMinuteBuys,
 894:             int fiveMinuteSignals, int fiveMinuteBuys,
 895:             int oneHourSignals, int oneHourBuys,
 896:             int buys, int watches, int sells, int strongSells, int errors,
 897:             java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals
 898:     ) {
 899:         int total() { return oneMinuteSignals + fiveMinuteSignals + oneHourSignals; }
 900:     }
 901: 
 902:     private List<Candle> candlesInExecutionWindow(List<Candle> candles, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 903:         return candles.stream()
 904:                 .filter(c -> {
 905:                     Instant t = c.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval));
 906:                     return !t.isBefore(start) && !t.isAfter(end);
 907:                 })
 908:                 .toList();
 909:     }
 910: 
 911:     private int countSignals(List<TradeSignal> signals, String interval) {
 912:         return (int) signals.stream().filter(s -> interval.equals(s.getInterval())).count();
 913:     }
 914: 
 915:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 916:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 917:     }
 918: 
 919:     private void updateRun(long runId, String status, int progress, String step, String error) {
 920:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 921:                 UPDATE analysis_test_run
 922:                 SET status=?, progress_percent=?, current_step=?, error_message=?,
 923:                     started_at=COALESCE(started_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)), heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 924:                 WHERE id=?
 925:                 """, status, Math.max(0, Math.min(100, progress)), step, error, runId);
 926:     }
 927: 
 928: 
 929: 
 930:     private String currentStep(long runId) {
 931:         try {
 932:             return jdbcTemplate.queryForObject(
 933:                     "SELECT current_step FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", String.class, runId);
 934:         } catch (Exception ignored) {
 935:             return "Unknown regression phase";
 936:         }
 937:     }
 938: 
 939:     private Throwable rootCause(Throwable exception) {
 940:         if (exception == null) return null;
 941:         Throwable root = exception;
 942:         while (root.getCause() != null && root.getCause() != root) {
 943:             root = root.getCause();
 944:         }
 945:         return root;
 946:     }
 947: 
 948:     private String rootCauseDetail(Throwable root, int max) {
 949:         if (root == null) return "Unknown root cause";
 950:         String message = root.getMessage();
 951:         String detail = root.getClass().getName() + (message == null || message.isBlank() ? "" : ": " + message);
 952:         return abbreviate(detail, max);
 953:     }
 954: 
 955:     private String stackTrace(Throwable exception) {
 956:         if (exception == null) return "Unknown regression test error";
 957:         StringWriter buffer = new StringWriter();
 958:         try (PrintWriter writer = new PrintWriter(buffer)) {
 959:             exception.printStackTrace(writer);
 960:         }
 961:         return buffer.toString();
 962:     }
 963: 
 964:     private Instant toInstant(Object value) {
 965:         if (value instanceof Timestamp timestamp) return timestamp.toInstant();
 966:         if (value instanceof Instant instant) return instant;
 967:         if (value instanceof LocalDateTime localDateTime) return localDateTime.toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
 968:         throw new IllegalStateException("Unsupported regression timestamp value: " + value);
 969:     }
 970: 
 971: 
 972:     private String errorDetail(Throwable exception, int max) {
 973:         if (exception == null) return "Unknown regression test error";
 974:         Throwable root = exception;
 975:         while (root.getCause() != null && root.getCause() != root) {
 976:             root = root.getCause();
 977:         }
 978:         StringBuilder detail = new StringBuilder()
 979:                 .append(exception.getClass().getName())
 980:                 .append(": ")
 981:                 .append(exception.getMessage() == null ? exception.toString() : exception.getMessage());
 982:         if (root != exception) {
 983:             detail.append(" | Root cause: ")
 984:                     .append(root.getClass().getName())
 985:                     .append(": ")
 986:                     .append(root.getMessage() == null ? root.toString() : root.getMessage());
 987:         }
 988:         return abbreviate(detail.toString(), max);
 989:     }
 990: 
 991:     private String abbreviate(String value, int max) {
 992:         if (value == null) return "Unknown regression test error";
 993:         return value.length() <= max ? value : value.substring(0, max);
 994:     }
 995: 
 996: 
 997: }

  21:     private final RestClient restClient;
  22:     private final RestClient timeClient;
  23:     private final RestClient reconciliationClient;

  26:     public BinanceMarketDataClient(
  27:         RestClient.Builder restClientBuilder,
  28:         BinanceMarketDataProperties properties
  29:     ) {
  30:         this.properties = properties;
  31:         int requestTimeoutMs=Math.max(1_000,properties.getRequestTimeoutSeconds()*1_000);
  32:         var marketFactory=new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
  33:         marketFactory.setConnectTimeout(requestTimeoutMs);marketFactory.setReadTimeout(requestTimeoutMs);
  34:         this.restClient = restClientBuilder
  35:             .baseUrl(properties.getBaseUrl()).requestFactory(marketFactory)
  36:             .build();
  37:         // Clock sampling must not delay application startup for the general
  38:         // historical-request timeout. This client is used only for /api/v3/time.
  39:         int clockTimeoutMs=Math.max(1_000,Math.min(5_000,properties.getRequestTimeoutSeconds()*1_000));
  40:         var clockFactory=new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
  41:         clockFactory.setConnectTimeout(clockTimeoutMs);clockFactory.setReadTimeout(clockTimeoutMs);
  42:         this.timeClient=restClientBuilder.baseUrl(properties.getBaseUrl()).requestFactory(clockFactory).build();
  43:         // Recovery is bounded by the drain window.  It must not spend the
  44:         // general 30-second historical-request timeout per candidate.
  45:         var recoveryFactory=new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
  46:         recoveryFactory.setConnectTimeout(clockTimeoutMs);recoveryFactory.setReadTimeout(clockTimeoutMs);
  47:         this.reconciliationClient=restClientBuilder.baseUrl(properties.getBaseUrl()).requestFactory(recoveryFactory).build();
  48:     }
  49: 
  50:     public List<BinanceKline> getKlines(
  51:         String symbol,
  52:         String interval,
  53:         int limit
  54:     ) {
  55:         validateRequest(symbol, interval, limit);
  56: 
  57:         List<List<Object>> response = restClient.get()
  58:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder
  59:                 .path("/api/v3/klines")
  60:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
  61:                 .queryParam("interval", interval)
  62:                 .queryParam("limit", limit)
  63:                 .build())
  64:             .retrieve()
  65:             .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {
  66:             });
  67: 
  68:         if (response == null || response.isEmpty()) {
  69:             return List.of();
  70:         }
  71: 
  72:         List<BinanceKline> candles = new ArrayList<>(response.size());
  73: 
  74:         for (List<Object> row : response) {
  75:             candles.add(mapKline(row));
  76:         }
  77: 
  78:         return candles;
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     /** Exact historical lookup used only by the local reconciliation fallback.
  82:      * The kline payload does not carry symbol/interval, so caller context is
  83:      * authoritative and returned open/close timestamps are validated by the
  84:      * reconciliation service. */
  85:     public List<BinanceKline> getKlines(String symbol, String interval, Instant startTime, Instant endTime) {
  86:         if (startTime == null || endTime == null || endTime.isBefore(startTime))
  87:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Valid kline time range is required");
  88:         validateRequest(symbol, interval, 2);
  89:         List<List<Object>> response = reconciliationClient.get()
  90:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder.path("/api/v3/klines")
  91:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
  92:                 .queryParam("interval", interval)
  93:                 .queryParam("startTime", startTime.toEpochMilli())
  94:                 .queryParam("endTime", endTime.toEpochMilli())
  95:                 .queryParam("limit", 2).build())
  96:             .retrieve().body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {});
  97:         if (response == null || response.isEmpty()) return List.of();
  98:         List<BinanceKline> candles = new ArrayList<>(response.size());
  99:         for (List<Object> row : response) candles.add(mapKline(row));
 100:         return candles;
 101:     }

 103:     /** Bounded probe used only to distinguish a pre-listing identity from a
 104:      * transient empty exact-range response. */
 105:     public List<BinanceKline> getKlinesAtOrAfter(String symbol, String interval, Instant startTime) {
 106:         if(startTime==null)throw new IllegalArgumentException("startTime is required");
 107:         validateRequest(symbol,interval,1);
 108:         List<List<Object>> response=reconciliationClient.get().uri(uriBuilder->uriBuilder.path("/api/v3/klines")
 109:             .queryParam("symbol",symbol.toUpperCase()).queryParam("interval",interval)
 110:             .queryParam("startTime",startTime.toEpochMilli()).queryParam("limit",1).build())
 111:             .retrieve().body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {});
 112:         if(response==null||response.isEmpty())return List.of();
 113:         return response.stream().map(this::mapKline).toList();
 114:     }
 115: 
 116:     /** Exchange time is required when deciding whether a REST kline is final. */
 117:     public Instant getServerTime() {
 118:         Map<String,Object> response=timeClient.get().uri("/api/v3/time").retrieve()
 119:             .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {});
 120:         if(response==null || !(response.get("serverTime") instanceof Number serverTime))
 121:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance time response did not contain serverTime");
 122:         return Instant.ofEpochMilli(serverTime.longValue());
 123:     }

 125:     public BinanceOrderBook getOrderBook(String symbol, int limit) {
 126:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 127:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 128:         }
 129:         if (limit < 1 || limit > 1000) {
 130:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Depth limit must be between 1 and 1000");
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         BinanceOrderBook response = restClient.get()
 134:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder
 135:                 .path("/api/v3/depth")
 136:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
 137:                 .queryParam("limit", limit)
 138:                 .build())
 139:             .retrieve()
 140:             .body(BinanceOrderBook.class);
 141: 
 142:         if (response == null) {
 143:             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty Binance order-book response for " + symbol);
 144:         }
 145:         return response;
 146:     }

 148: 
 149:     /**
 150:      * FIX-11T: read the symbol's current Binance minimum executable notional directly
 151:      * from exchangeInfo.  Near-TP uses this only as a minimum-order guard; it does not
 152:      * introduce a separate quantity-rounding model. NOTIONAL is preferred when present,
 153:      * with MIN_NOTIONAL retained for symbols that still expose the older filter.
 154:      */
 155:     public BigDecimal getMinimumExecutableNotional(String symbol) {
 156:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 157:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 158:         }
 159: 
 160:         Map<String, Object> response = restClient.get()
 161:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder
 162:                 .path("/api/v3/exchangeInfo")
 163:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
 164:                 .build())
 165:             .retrieve()
 166:             .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {
 167:             });
 168: 
 169:         if (response == null) {
 170:             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty Binance exchangeInfo response for " + symbol);
 171:         }
 172:         Object symbolsValue = response.get("symbols");
 173:         if (!(symbolsValue instanceof List<?> symbols) || symbols.isEmpty() || !(symbols.get(0) instanceof Map<?, ?> symbolInfo)) {
 174:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain symbol rules for " + symbol);
 175:         }
 176:         Object filtersValue = symbolInfo.get("filters");
 177:         if (!(filtersValue instanceof List<?> filters)) {
 178:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain filters for " + symbol);
 179:         }
 180: 
 181:         BigDecimal minNotional = null;
 182:         for (Object filterValue : filters) {
 183:             if (!(filterValue instanceof Map<?, ?> filter)) continue;
 184:             String filterType = String.valueOf(filter.get("filterType"));
 185:             if (!"NOTIONAL".equals(filterType) && !"MIN_NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) continue;
 186:             Object raw = filter.get("minNotional");
 187:             if (raw == null) continue;
 188:             BigDecimal candidate = new BigDecimal(String.valueOf(raw));
 189:             if (candidate.signum() <= 0) continue;
 190:             if ("NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) return candidate;
 191:             minNotional = candidate;
 192:         }
 193:         if (minNotional != null) return minNotional;
 194:         throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not expose a minimum notional for " + symbol);
 195:     }
 196: 
 197:     private BinanceKline mapKline(List<Object> row) {
 198:         if (row == null || row.size() < 11) {
 199:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
 200:                 "Invalid Binance kline response"
 201:             );
 202:         }
 203: 
 204:         return new BinanceKline(
 205:             Instant.ofEpochMilli(toLong(row.get(0))),
 206:             toDecimal(row.get(1)),
 207:             toDecimal(row.get(2)),
 208:             toDecimal(row.get(3)),
 209:             toDecimal(row.get(4)),
 210:             toDecimal(row.get(5)),
 211:             Instant.ofEpochMilli(toLong(row.get(6))),
 212:             toDecimal(row.get(7)),
 213:             toLong(row.get(8)),
 214:             toDecimal(row.get(9)),
 215:             toDecimal(row.get(10))
 216:         );
 217:     }
 218: 
 219:     private BigDecimal toDecimal(Object value) {
 220:         return new BigDecimal(String.valueOf(value));
 221:     }
 222: 
 223:     private long toLong(Object value) {
 224:         if (value instanceof Number number) {
 225:             return number.longValue();
 226:         }
 227: 
 228:         return Long.parseLong(String.valueOf(value));
 229:     }
 230: 
 231:     private void validateRequest(
 232:         String symbol,
 233:         String interval,
 234:         int limit
 235:     ) {
 236:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 237:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 238:         }
 239: 
 240:         if (interval == null || interval.isBlank()) {
 241:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Interval is required");
 242:         }
 243: 
 244:         if (limit < 1 || limit > 1000) {
 245:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
 246:                 "Limit must be between 1 and 1000"
 247:             );
 248:         }
 249:     }
 250: }

  23: /** FIX-131: one owner for startup/reload/health. The actor never does database or socket I/O.
  24:  * A timed-out, unresolved handshake is retained: cancellation alone cannot prove no late socket.
  25:  * Availability is deliberately sacrificed until old work AND transport have terminated. */
  26: @Component
  27: public class BinanceWebSocketManager {
  28:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  29:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  30: 
  31:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(BinanceWebSocketManager.class);
  32:     static final long SCAN_NANOS = TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(500);
  33:     static final long DEADLINE_NANOS = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(15);
  34:     interface Connector { CompletableFuture<WebSocketSession> connect(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, String url); }
  35:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  36:     private final Supplier<String> urls;

  38:     private final BinanceKlineService service;
  39:     private final Connector connector;
  40:     private final CandleGapDiagnostics gaps;
  41:     @Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.LocalCandleReconciliationService reconciliation;
  42:     private final java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos;
  43:     private final ArrayBlockingQueue<CompletableFuture<Void>> requests = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(32);
  44:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor connectWorker = worker("fix131-connect", 1);
  45:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor closeWorker = worker("fix131-close", 1);
  46:     private final Object lifecycle = new Object();
  47:     private final CompletableFuture<Void> stopped = new CompletableFuture<>();
  48:     private final List<CompletableFuture<Void>> waiting = new ArrayList<>();
  49:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleanup = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  50:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleaning = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  51:     private volatile boolean stopRequested;
  52:     private volatile Thread actor;
  53:     private volatile String phase = "STOPPED";
  54:     private volatile Attempt current;
  55:     private long sequence;
  56:     private long interruptionStart;
  57:     private Instant revokedAt;
  58:     private long nextAttempt;
  59:     private boolean replacement;
  60:     private static final class Attempt {
  61:         final BinanceWebSocketHandler handler;
  62:         final long started;
  63:         volatile boolean settled;
  64:         volatile Throwable failure;
  65:         volatile String url;
  66:         volatile boolean closing;
  67:         long nextClose;

  69:         boolean timeoutLogged;
  70:         long nextSilenceLog;
  71:         Attempt(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, long started) { this.handler = handler; this.started = started; }
  72:     }
  73:     @Autowired

  75:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  76:         this(properties, builder::build, mapper, service,
  77:                 (handler, url) -> new StandardWebSocketClient().execute(handler, null, URI.create(url)), gaps);
  78:     }
  79:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,

  83:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  84:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps,
  85:             java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos) {
  86:         this.nanos = nanos;

  88:         this.connector = connector; this.gaps = gaps;
  89:     }
  90:     private static ThreadPoolExecutor worker(String name, int queue) {
  91:         return new ThreadPoolExecutor(1, 1, 0, TimeUnit.SECONDS, new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queue), task -> {
  92:             Thread t = new Thread(task, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t;
  93:         }, new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  94:     }
  95:     @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
  96:     public void start() {
  97:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  98:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
  99:             if (actor != null || stopRequested || !properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) return;
 100:             replacement = true;
 101:             actor = new Thread(this::run, "fix131-ws-owner");
 102:             actor.setDaemon(true);
 103:             actor.start();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106:     void wake() { LockSupport.unpark(actor); }
 107:     private void notice(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler) {
 108:         if (handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED && !handler.allClosed()) {
 109:             if (current == null || current.handler != handler) cleanup.add(handler);
 110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.
 111:             // This fallback runs only during shutdown, never on a live processing callback.
 112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();
 113:         }
 114:         wake();
 115:     }
 116:     CompletableFuture<Void> requestReload() {
 117:         if (!properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 118:         var request = new CompletableFuture<Void>();
 119:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 120:             if (stopRequested) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 121:             if (!requests.offer(request)) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY);
 122:         }
 123:         start(); wake();
 124:         return request;
 125:     }
 126:     public void reload() {
 127:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
 128:         var request = requestReload();
 129:         try { request.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 130:         catch (TimeoutException ex) { throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 131:         catch (InterruptedException ex) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 132:         catch (ExecutionException ex) {
 133:             if (ex.getCause() instanceof RuntimeException runtime) throw runtime;
 134:             throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 135:         }
 136:     }
 137:     private WebSocketReconnectException failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 138:         return new WebSocketReconnectException(reason, phase);
 139:     }
 140:     private void run() {
 141:         try {
 142:             while (true) {
 143:                 // Persistent lifecycle facts are examined on EVERY pass, before more queued requests.
 144:                 tick();
 145:                 if (stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty()) break;
 146:                 LockSupport.parkNanos(SCAN_NANOS);
 147:             }
 148:             phase = "STOPPED";
 149:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 150:             stopped.complete(null);
 151:         } catch (Throwable ex) {
 152:             log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][OWNER_FAILED] phase={}; restart required", phase, ex);
 153:             synchronized (lifecycle) { stopRequested = true; if (current != null) current.handler.gate.revoke(); }
 154:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 155:             stopped.completeExceptionally(ex);
 156:         }
 157:     }
 158:     private void tick() {
 159:         long now = nanos.getAsLong();
 160:         for (var h : cleanup) {
 161:             if (h.allClosed()) { cleanup.remove(h); continue; }
 162:             if (h.gate.isDrained() && cleaning.add(h)) {
 163:                 try { closeWorker.execute(() -> {
 164:                     try { h.close(); } finally { cleaning.remove(h); wake(); }
 165:                 }); } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { cleaning.remove(h); }
 166:             }
 167:         }
 168:         // Bound callers retained off-mailbox as well as mailbox capacity.
 169:         CompletableFuture<Void> request;
 170:         for (int n = 0; n < 32 && (request = requests.poll()) != null; n++) {
 171:             if (waiting.size() >= 32) request.completeExceptionally(failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY));
 172:             else { waiting.add(request); replacement = true; }
 173:         }
 174:         if (current != null) {
 175:             var a = current;
 176:             var h = a.handler;
 177:             if (a.failure != null && h.gate.state() != GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 178:                 log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTION_FAILED] generation={}", h.generation, a.failure);
 179:                 finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 180:             }
 181:             if (stopRequested || replacement || h.failed || a.failure != null
 182:                     || (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.ADMITTING && !h.isConnected())) revoke(a, now);
 183:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.PENDING) {
 184:                 if (now - a.started >= DEADLINE_NANOS) {
 185:                     log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTION_TIMEOUT] generation={}; awaiting terminal handshake/closure", h.generation);
 186:                     revoke(a, now);
 187:                     finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 188:                 } else if (a.settled && h.isConnected()) {
 189:                     synchronized (lifecycle) {
 190:                         if (!stopRequested && h.gate.activate()) {
 191:                             phase = "ACTIVE";
 192:                             h.lastReceiptNanos = System.nanoTime();
 193:                             log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][ADMISSION_ENABLED] generation={}, admittedAt={}, revokedAt={}, interruptionMs={}",
 194:                                     h.generation, Instant.now(), revokedAt, interruptionStart == 0 ? 0 : TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(now - interruptionStart));
 195:                             interruptionStart = 0;
 196:                             try { if (gaps != null) gaps.subscribed(a.url, Instant.now()); }
 197:                             catch (RuntimeException ex) { log.warn("[FIX-131][SUBSCRIPTION_DIAGNOSTIC_FAILED] generation={}", h.generation, ex); }
 198:                             // Request only; all local queries and REST recovery run on
 199:                             // the dedicated reconciliation executor, never this actor.
 200:                             try { if(reconciliation!=null) reconciliation.requestAfterReconnect(h.generation); }
 201:                             catch(RuntimeException ex) { log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REQUEST_FAILED] generation={}",h.generation,ex); }
 202:                             finish(null);
 203:                         }
 204:                     }
 205:                 }
 206:             }
 207:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.ADMITTING && now >= a.nextSilenceLog && System.nanoTime() - h.lastReceiptNanos > TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30)) {
 208:                 a.nextSilenceLog = now + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
 209:                 log.warn("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][NO_MESSAGES] generation={}, silenceMs={}; observe only, no forced reconnect", h.generation, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - h.lastReceiptNanos));
 210:             }
 211:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 212:                 phase = h.gate.isDrained() ? "CLOSING" : "DRAINING";
 213:                 if (now - a.revoked >= DEADLINE_NANOS && !a.timeoutLogged) {
 214:                     a.timeoutLogged = true;
 215:                     var reason = h.gate.isDrained() ? WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CLOSE_TIMEOUT : WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.DRAIN_TIMEOUT;
 216:                     log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][{}] generation={}, inFlight={}; replacement remains blocked", reason, h.generation, h.gate.inFlight());
 217:                     finish(reason);
 218:                 }
 219:                 if (h.gate.isDrained()) {
 220:                     if (!h.allClosed() && !a.closing && now >= a.nextClose) {
 221:                         a.closing = true;
 222:                         try { closeWorker.execute(() -> { try { h.close(); } finally { a.closing = false; wake(); } }); }
 223:                         catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { a.closing = false; log.error("[FIX-131][CLOSE_WORKER_REJECTED] generation={}", h.generation); }
 224:                         a.nextClose = now + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
 225:                     }
 226:                     if (a.settled && h.allClosed() && !a.closing) {
 227:                         log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][RETIRED] generation={}, drainedAt={}, rejectedMessages={}", h.generation, h.gate.drainedAt(), h.rejected.get());
 228:                         current = null;
 229:                         replacement = !stopRequested;
 230:                         nextAttempt = now + (a.failure == null ? 0 : TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(Math.max(1, properties.getWebsocket().getHealthCheckSeconds())));
 231:                     }
 232:                 }
 233:             }
 234:         }
 235:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 236:             if (!stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty() && replacement && now >= nextAttempt) launch();
 237:         }
 238:     }
 239:     private void revoke(Attempt a, long now) {
 240:         if (a.handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) return;

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java:1-180

  17: @Service
  18: public class MarketDataService {
  19:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  20:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  21: 
  22: 
  23:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
  24:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  25:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  26: 
  27:     public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
  28:         this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:     }
  30: 
  31:     public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  32:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
  33:         String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  34:         // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
  35:         // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
  36:         // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
  37:         java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
  38:         List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
  39:         // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
  40:         // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
  41:         Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
  42:             int count=0;
  43:             for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
  44:                 // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
  45:                 if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
  46:                 count += candleRepository.upsert(
  47:                         normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
  48:                         kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
  49:                         kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
  50:                         kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
  51:             }
  52:             return count;
  53:         });
  54:         if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
  55:         return upserted;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
  60:         return candleRepository
  61:                 .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
  62:                         symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
  63:                 .stream()
  64:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
  65:                 .toList();
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
  69:         return Candle.builder()
  70:                 .symbol(symbol)
  71:                 .intervalCode(interval)
  72:                 .openTime(kline.openTime())
  73:                 .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
  74:                 .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
  75:                 .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
  76:                 .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
  77:                 .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
  78:                 .volume(kline.volume())
  79:                 .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
  80:                 .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
  81:                 .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
  82:                 .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
  83:                 .closed(true)
  84:                 .build();
  85:     }
  86: }

  99:     public int upsert(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 100:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 101:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 102:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed) {
 103:         source.requireLocalWriter();

 105:             volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"BACKFILL",null);

 107:     /** FIX-153: result is valid only after the caller's INPUT transaction commits.
 108:      * Token read-back compares immutable receipt identity, never a racy pre-read of closed state. */
 109:     public Transition upsertRuntime(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 110:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 111:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 112:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String token) {
 113:         source.requireLocalWriter();

 115:             volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"RUNTIME",closed?token:null);
 116:         return local.queryForObject("SELECT closed,close_time,close_transition_token,ingestion_source FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",

 118:             // the live stream closes is a genuine close transition; BACKFILL never writes
 119:             // a token, so it cannot manufacture a dispatchable transition.
 120:             (rs,n)->new Transition(closed && token.equals(rs.getString(3)),

 122:     }
 123:     /** `alreadyClosed` is diagnostic only; the matching token is the sole dispatch authority. */
 124:     public record Transition(boolean transitionedToClosed,Instant closeTime,String token,boolean alreadyClosed) {}

 126:     /**
 127:      * Authoritative recovery is intentionally update-only.  Unlike ordinary
 128:      * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
 129:      * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
 130:      * live close admission.
 131:      */
 132:     public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 133:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 134:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 135:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {

 139:               quote_asset_volume=?,number_of_trades=?,taker_buy_base_volume=?,taker_buy_quote_volume=?,

 142:               AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')

 144:             numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
 145:     }

 147:     /**

 164:                 closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume) == 1;
 165:         } catch (org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {

 169:     private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 170:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 171:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 172:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String provenance,String token) {
 173:         String duplicateClause = "BACKFILL".equals(provenance) ? """
 174:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 175:                 -- REST/backfill is authoritative enough to finish an interrupted open
 176:                 -- candle, but never changes a completed candle, provenance or token.
 177:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 178:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 179:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 180:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),

 185:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),

 187:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 188:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 189:             """ : """
 190:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE

 192:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 193:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 194:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 195:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 196:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 197:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 198:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 199:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 200:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 201:                 close_transition_token=IF(closed=0 AND VALUES(closed)=1,VALUES(close_transition_token),close_transition_token),
 202:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 203:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 204:             """;
 205:         // FIX-153: backfill inserts missing identities only, so it cannot fabricate a runtime close.
 206:         return local.update("""
 207:             INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 208:                 volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,

  23:  * when the application starts. Existing candles are preserved and only missing
  24:  * Binance rows are inserted by MarketDataService.
  25:  */
  26: @Component
  27: @Order(10)

  31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")

  33:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}")

  38:

  40:     private final TradingProperties tradingProperties;

  42:     private final MarketDataService marketDataService;

  48:     @Override
  49:     public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
  50:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  51:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
  52:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
  53:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap is disabled");
  54:             return;
  55:         }
  56: 
  57:         List<String> symbols = selectSymbols();
  58:         List<String> intervals = selectIntervals();
  59:         int limit = Math.max(
  60:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
  61:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
  62:         );
  63: 
  64:         for (String symbol : symbols) {
  65:             for (String interval : intervals) {
  66:                 bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
  67:             }
  68:         }
  69:     }
  70: 
  71: 
  72:     public void bootstrapSymbol(String rawSymbol) {
  73:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  74:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
  75:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
  76:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap skipped for {} because historical loading is disabled", rawSymbol);
  77:             return;
  78:         }
  79: 
  80:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();
  81:         if (symbol.isBlank()) {
  82:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
  83:         }
  84: 
  85:         int limit = Math.max(
  86:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
  87:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
  88:         );
  89:         for (String interval : selectIntervals()) {
  90:             bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
  91:         }
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     private void bootstrap(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  95:         try {
  96:             int inserted = marketDataService.importCandles(symbol, interval, limit);
  97: 
  98:             log.info(
  99:                     "Historical bootstrap completed: symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}",
 100:                     symbol,
 101:                     interval,
 102:                     inserted
 103:             );
 104: 
 105:             // FIX-153: bootstrap is data only. An imported close must never seed a signal
 106:             // or execute a STARTUP trade; only this trader's runtime closes qualify in OFF.
 107:             if("OFF".equals(marketMode)){
 108:                 log.info("[FIX-153][BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED] symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}, analysis=false, execution=false",symbol,interval,inserted);
 109:                 return;
 110:             }
 111:             TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 112:                     .calculateAndPersist(symbol, interval, null)
 113:                     .orElse(null);
 114: 
 115:             if (indicator == null) {
 116:                 log.warn(
 117:                         "Initial indicator skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, reason=fewer than 210 closed candles",
 118:                         symbol,
 119:                         interval
 120:                 );
 121:                 return;
 122:             }
 123: 
 124:             // FIX-072: bootstrap can overlap with the live candle-close worker during startup.
 125:             // Never regenerate or re-execute a signal that Production already persisted for this
 126:             // exact symbol/interval/candle; the unique key remains the final database safety net.
 127:             if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 128:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {
 129:                 log.info(
 130:                         "Initial signal skipped: already exists for symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}",
 131:                         indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime());
 132:                 return;
 133:             }
 134: 
 135:             TradeSignal signal = analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.STARTUP);
 136:             paperTradingService.processSignal(signal);
 137: 
 138:             log.info(
 139:                     "Initial signal generated: symbol={}, interval={}, score={}, decision={}",
 140:                     symbol,
 141:                     interval,
 142:                     signal.getTotalScore(),
 143:                     signal.getDecision()
 144:             );
 145:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 146:             log.error(
 147:                     "Historical bootstrap failed: symbol={}, interval={}",
 148:                     symbol,
 149:                     interval,
 150:                     exception
 151:             );
 152:         }
 153:     }
 154: 
 155:     private List<String> selectSymbols() {
 156:         Set<String> values = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 157:         values.addAll(coinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols());
 158:         return List.copyOf(values);
 159:     }
