### codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:1-180
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture
   2: 
   3: Status labels used in this document:
   4: 
   5: - **Current** ƒ?" present in the repository today.
   6: - **Pending** ƒ?" approved design direction, not implemented or deployed.
   7: - **Legacy/shared** ƒ?" existing shared-market path retained as historical evidence
   8:   while the trader moves to local-candle ownership.
   9: 
  10: ## Current release status
  11: 
  12: **FIX-153 is implemented in source but is NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION.**
  13: It moves the trader toward collector-free, trader-owned local candle ingestion
  14: in `shared-market.mode=OFF`. Java 21, disposable-MySQL, crash/race, Windows
  15: two-process, soak, OFF-mode and Replay-parity proof gates remain open.
  16: 
  17: FIX-154 is a separate pending higher-timeframe design. It is not implemented
  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.
  19: 
  20: ## System purpose
  21: 
  22: The application is a Spring Boot cryptocurrency trader that analyses Binance
  23: market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,
  24: and performs paper-wallet execution and position protection. Production and
  25: Replay are intended to use equivalent persisted market evidence.
  26: 
  27: ## Runtime and data ownership
  28: 
  29: | Area | Current ownership | Target direction |
  30: |---|---|---|
  31: | Trader database | `crypto_ai` | Remains the trader's only runtime database. |
  32: | Collector database | `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector remains separate and is not read/written by the trader at runtime. |
  33: | Candle ingestion | In OFF mode, trader-owned Binance ingestion writes local `crypto_ai.candle`. | Must be proven in a collector-free deployment rehearsal. |
  34: | Candle processing | Bounded lanes, close-transition tokens and a local detector are implemented. | MySQL/race/crash proof remains required. |
  35: | Trading | Paper wallet and managed positions. | Live-exchange orders are outside the local-candle automatic-resolution design. |
  36: 
  37: ## Current primary flow
  38: 
  39: ```text
  40: Binance WebSocket kline
  41:   -> BinanceKlineService
  42:   -> KlineTransactionCoordinator
  43:       -> input transaction: candle + canonical 1m market price event
  44:       -> protection transaction: LivePositionProtectionService
  45:       -> observer transaction
  46:       -> close-dispatch transaction
  47:   -> CandleClosedEvent (AFTER_COMMIT)
  48:   -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher
  49:   -> CandleClosedAnalysisWorker
  50:   -> TechnicalIndicatorService
  51:   -> AnalysisService / TradeSignal
  52:   -> SignalProcessingCoordinator
  53:   -> PaperTradingService / wallet and managed-position state
  54: ```
  55: 
  56: `KlineTransactionCoordinator` preserves input -> protection -> observer ->
  57: close-dispatch ordering. Closed-candle analysis is asynchronous and must not
  58: run on the Binance ingestion thread.
  59: 
  60: ## Current core persistence
  61: 
  62: | Table/domain | Purpose |
  63: |---|---|
  64: | `candle` | Local OHLCV candle history keyed by symbol, interval, and open time. |
  65: | `technical_indicator` | Persisted indicator snapshots derived from closed candles. |
  66: | `trade_signal` | Persisted analysis decisions and their context. |
  67: | `signal_processing_work` | Durable processing registration/status for a persisted signal. |
  68: | `market_price_event` | Canonical 1m price evidence used by protection and Replay. |
  69: | Paper wallet / position tables | Paper executions, open-position state, TP/SL/profit protection. |
  70: 
  71: ## Legacy/shared-market path
  72: 
  73: **Current but pending retirement from the trader runtime.**
  74: 
  75: The shared path includes `SharedMarketSource`, `SharedMarketConsumer`,
  76: `SharedAnalysisDispatcher`, cutover/readiness logic, and local historical
  77: tables such as `shared_market_event_delivery`, `shared_market_consumer_state`,
  78: and `shared_market_health`. These records remain evidence and must not be
  79: dropped, reset, or used to manufacture an approval.
  80: 
  81: The collector event/cursor source is `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`
  82: and `market_data_stream_cursor`. In the target local-candle runtime, the
  83: trader must not connect to, read, or write these collector tables.
  84: 
  85: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` is an existing Java Flyway migration
  86: that requires the collector event table for a fresh installation. A trader-only
  87: bootstrap policy is therefore **pending** and remains a release blocker. Do
  88: not edit applied Flyway migrations or Flyway history.
  89: 
  90: ## Implemented, unverified: local-candle architecture (FIX-153)
  91: 
  92: The implemented intended mode is `shared-market.mode=OFF` with trader-owned Binance kline
  93: ingestion and local candle writes. The shared executors and direct-ingestion
  94: path must never run together.
  95: 
  96: Implemented safeguards:
  97: 
  98: - Candle writes are monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or overwrite a
  99:   closed candle.
 100: - A closed-candle transition is admitted exactly once using an atomic upsert
 101:   result plus in-process admission coordination.
 102: - A bounded local detector recovers a closed candle that was committed before
 103:   the in-memory event/dispatcher could start analysis.
 104: - Fresh candles may execute; stale candles are analysed historically only, or
 105:   their already-owned work is expired without business execution.
 106: - Analysis lanes are bounded. Under overload, 1m may retain only the latest
 107:   pending candle; higher intervals use an explicit bounded FIFO policy.
 108: - Historic shared `REVIEW_REQUIRED` evidence must not block OFF-mode work.
 109: - OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must use local-candle health and must not
 110:   query retired shared-market tables.
 111: - Current REST backfill can finalise an interrupted open row but cannot mint a
 112:   runtime close token or dispatch analysis. Runtime close tokens plus
 113:   provenance keep backfill out of detector admission. The approved, pending
 114:   automatic-reconciliation extension will also insert an exact wholly absent
 115:   candle only inside the active quality window, with `BACKFILL` provenance and
 116:   the same no-token/no-dispatch boundary.
 117: - Observed local behavior (2026-10-09): a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close
 118:   boundary can leave the already-persisted 1m/5m rows open because Binance does
 119:   not replay the final closed-kline event. The current detector recovers missing
 120:   analysis admission, not missing close facts. An authoritative, bounded REST
 121:   repair may therefore finalise the exact `closed=0` row or, under the approved
 122:   gap-backfill scope, insert an exact absent identity. It must preserve existing
 123:   provenance, use `BACKFILL` for an insertion, create no close-transition token,
 124:   and never dispatch historical work. Automatic reconciliation of this reconnect
 125:   case remains a FIX-153 release gate; the one-time local repair is evidence,
 126:   not proof that the gap is closed.
 127: - Approved pending reconciliation design: gap backfill is insert-only (no
 128:   duplicate-key update); it can never alter a live row that appears during a
 129:   REST request. Candidate selection is bounded to the active quality window;
 130:   terminal audit records are not retried. Every candle/work route must produce
 131:   a durable terminal outcome or remain explicitly retryable after a transient
 132:   failure. Historical-only processing has no execution authority.
 133: - The pending universal mutation barrier is an in-memory drain participant,
 134:   not a database or persistent trading fence. Outside drain it must not delay,
 135:   queue, or block fresh trading, signals, or protection. Open-position
 136:   protection during application replacement remains an unresolved release gate.
 137: - Local source now contains the first reconciliation implementation slice:
 138:   V107 durable audit/outcome tables, update-only authoritative open-row repair,
 139:   pure insert-only gap backfill, a dedicated recovery executor, a reconnect
 140:   request hook, and drain participation. It is disabled by default and remains
 141:   unvalidated on MySQL; it has no deployment approval.
 142: - Direct prices are normalised to `DECIMAL(30,12)` before persistence,
 143:   protection and completion. Equal timestamps have one canonical receipt.
 144: - Every processing attempt is freshness-checked after normal locks using the
 145:   database-to-exchange correction. JVM-origin timestamp decisions use the
 146:   separately measured JVM-to-exchange correction. The measurement performs no
 147:   HTTP under execution locks and uses a bounded five-second time-only client.
 148:   General Binance REST calls use the configured request timeout, and scheduled
 149:   clock sampling has a four-thread Spring scheduler pool.
 150:   Bootstrap obtains time/klines before opening its local candle-write
 151:   transaction, so a stalled REST request does not retain database resources.
 152:   REST bootstrap gets Binance server time before klines.
 153: - An ADMIN-only drain stops analysis admission, exits the JVM on successful
 154:   quiesce, and resumes admission on timeout. Price protection pauses during
 155:   the controlled drain window.
 156: 
 157: ## Implemented, unverified: protection price-event state machine
 158: 
 159: For direct 1m price observations, the target evidence lifecycle is:
 160: 
 161: ```text
 162: PENDING
 163:   -> APPLIED                 (protection effects and state commit together)
 164:   -> NON_CANONICAL           (invalid, stale, out-of-order, or duplicate)
 165:   -> PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED  (confirmed rollback/crash resolution)
 166: ```
 167: 
 168: Replay and price authority consume only accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` rows.
 169: Replay must reject a parity claim when the requested range contains unresolved
 170: direct `PENDING` evidence; this must not block live trading.
 171: 
 172: The automatic resolver is valid only for the paper-trading phase, where every
 173: protection effect commits in the same transaction as its `APPLIED` update.
 174: Before deployment, the code must prove that there are no asynchronous,
 175: `REQUIRES_NEW`, in-memory-only, or external effects in this boundary. A future
 176: live-exchange order needs a separate idempotent/exchange-reconciliation design.
 177: 
 178: The target runtime also requires exactly one active trader process. This is an
 179: operational prerequisite, not a database lease or fence.
 180: 

### claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:1-180
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture (Claude)
   2: 
   3: Maintained by Claude. Based on a read-only review of the working tree on
   4: 2026-10-09 (branch `master`, including uncommitted FIX-153 changes). Line
   5: numbers are approximate. `codex-docs/` is maintained separately by Codex.
   6: 
   7: Status labels: **Current** (in the working tree), **Pending** (design only),
   8: **Legacy/shared** (retained shared-market path).
   9: 
  10: ## Release status
  11: 
  12: - FIX-153 (trader-owned local candles, `shared-market.mode=OFF`): implemented
  13:   in source, **not approved for production**. Java 21 build/tests, disposable
  14:   MySQL bootstrap, crash/race, Windows two-process, soak and Replay-parity
  15:   gates are open. This workstation has Java 17 and cannot compile the project.
  16: - FIX-153 work is **uncommitted**: ~67 modified tracked files plus untracked
  17:   `com/crypto/local/`, `com/crypto/infrastructure/diagnostics/`, migrations
  18:   `V99`ƒ?"`V106` (except the Java `V103`) and `B105`.
  19: - FIX-154 (higher-timeframe BUY validation): pending, not implemented.
  20: 
  21: ## Stack
  22: 
  23: Spring Boot 4.1.0, Java 21, MySQL 8 (`docker-compose.yml`: `mysql:8.4`,
  24: host port 3307), Flyway (`V1`ƒ?"`V106`, plus baseline `B105`), Spring Security
  25: form login, Jenkins + `scripts/deploy/Deploy-Trader.ps1`. Paper trading only;
  26: no live-exchange orders.
  27: 
  28: ## Databases
  29: 
  30: | Schema | Owner | Trader use |
  31: |---|---|---|
  32: | `crypto_ai` | Trader | Only runtime DB in OFF mode. |
  33: | `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector | Read in LIVE/OBSERVE via `SharedMarketSource` read-only pool. Must not be touched in OFF mode. |
  34: 
  35: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` (Java migration) adds indexes to
  36: `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`; a fresh trader-only install fails
  37: without that schema. Release blocker; do not edit applied migrations.
  38: 
  39: ## Ingestion modes (`shared-market.mode`, default `OFF`)
  40: 
  41: - **OFF (FIX-153)** ƒ?" trader connects to Binance and writes `crypto_ai.candle`.
  42:   `LocalTraderInstance` requires an absolute `local-candle.instance-lock-path`
  43:   (env `LOCAL_TRADER_INSTANCE_LOCK`) and holds an OS file lock: one trader
  44:   process per host. Empty value fails OFF-mode startup.
  45: - **LIVE / OBSERVE (legacy/shared)** ƒ?" `SharedMarketConsumer` polls collector
  46:   stream tables into local `shared_market_event_delivery`;
  47:   `SharedAnalysisDispatcher` drives analysis. Direct ingestion entry points
  48:   (`BinanceWebSocketManager`, `BinanceKlineService.processKline`,
  49:   `MarketDataService.importCandles`, `MarketDataBootstrapService`) are
  50:   disabled in LIVE. The two paths must never run together.
  51: 
  52: ## Primary flow (OFF mode)
  53: 
  54: ```text
  55: Binance WS (combined stream, enabled coins x 1m/5m/1h)
  56:  -> BinanceWebSocketManager (owner thread "fix131-ws-owner", generation gate)
  57:  -> BinanceWebSocketHandler.handleTextMessage (socket thread, synchronous)
  58:  -> BinanceKlineService.processKline        (@Transactional NEVER, drain check)
  59:  -> KlineTransactionCoordinator.process     (each phase REQUIRES_NEW, READ_COMMITTED, 30s)
  60:      1 INPUT        CandleRepository.upsertRuntime + MarketPriceEventService.recordPending (1m)
  61:      2 PROTECTION   LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice (1m; one retry on initial-lock deadlock)
  62:      3 OBSERVER     PriceMoveMonitorService.onPrice (errors swallowed)
  63:      4 CLOSE        publish CandleClosedEvent only if this receipt won the 0->1 close token
  64:  -> CandleClosedEventListener (AFTER_COMMIT, not @Async)
  65:  -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher (per symbol|interval lane)
  66:  -> candleAnalysisExecutor (core 8 / max 16 / queue 200, AbortPolicy)
  67:  -> CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.processShared
  68:  -> TechnicalIndicatorService -> AnalysisService -> TradeSignal + signal_processing_work
  69:  -> SignalProcessingCoordinator -> PaperTradingService -> WalletAutoExecutionService
  70: ```
  71: 
  72: All candle writes and live position protection run on the single WebSocket
  73: socket thread, one message at a time. Analysis never runs on it.
  74: 
  75: ## Candle persistence
  76: 
  77: - `CandleRepository.upsertRuntime/write`: `INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`
  78:   on `uk_candle_symbol_interval_open_time`. Once `closed=1` every column keeps
  79:   its old value (monotonic). RUNTIME writes set `close_transition_token` only
  80:   on the 0->1 transition; the read-back token equal to this receipt's UUID is
  81:   the sole authority to dispatch a close. BACKFILL writes never set a token.
  82: - `domain/Candle` is now a read DTO.
  83: 
  84: ## Recovery and gaps
  85: 
  86: - `LocalClosedCandleDetector` (every 2s, OFF only): re-admits tokened, non-
  87:   BACKFILL closed candles with no `trade_signal` and no
  88:   `signal_processing_work` (up to 6 retries). Repairs commit-to-dispatch gaps,
  89:   **not** missing close facts.
  90: - `CandleGapDiagnostics` (every 10s, 72h horizon): logs `DB_CANDLE_GAP` only,
  91:   `repair=false`.
  92: - REST backfill (`MarketDataService.importCandles`, BACKFILL provenance,
  93:   skips still-open candle, server time fetched before klines, no HTTP inside
  94:   the transaction) runs only at startup (`MarketDataBootstrapService`) or coin
  95:   activation.
  96: - **Gap:** a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close boundary can leave rows at
  97:   `closed=0` (Binance does not replay the final closed kline). Nothing repairs
  98:   this automatically; with `maximum-missing-candles: 0` it blocks analysis for
  99:   that symbol/interval. Deployment gate.
 100: 
 101: ## WebSocket lifecycle
 102: 
 103: `BinanceWebSocketManager` ticks every 500 ms on its owner thread; connect and
 104: close run on separate workers. Each attempt gets a new
 105: `BinanceWebSocketHandler` + `GenerationGate` (PENDING -> ADMITTING -> REVOKED);
 106: messages outside ADMITTING / the primary session are dropped. Handshake
 107: deadline 15s. Failure backoff = `websocket.health-check-seconds` (15s);
 108: `websocket.reconnect-delay-seconds` is only displayed by
 109: `BinanceStatusController` and does not drive reconnects. 30s silence only logs
 110: `NO_MESSAGES`. Coin changes call `reload()` (bounded mailbox, 30s wait).
 111: Symbols come from `CoinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols()` (DB), not yml.
 112: 
 113: ## Analysis
 114: 
 115: - `CandleAnalysisDispatcher`: one active task per lane. 1m keeps only the
 116:   newest pending candle (`PENDING_REPLACED`); higher intervals FIFO capped at
 117:   4 (`LANE_OVERFLOW`). Coordinates live and detector admissions.
 118: - `CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator`: 1024 striped locks by
 119:   symbol+interval+openTime; detector uses `tryLock`.
 120: - `CandleClosedAnalysisWorker` stages: retry resume -> coordination lock ->
 121:   `CandleDataQualityService` (>=210 candles, 0 gaps, valid OHLCV) ->
 122:   indicators -> existing-signal check -> freshness (`SharedEventPolicy`;
 123:   1m 90s, 5m 120s, 1h 300s, 4h 600s, 1d 1800s after close) -> LIVE analysis +
 124:   registration, or `HISTORICAL_ONLY` analysis for stale input -> signal
 125:   processing.
 126: - Signal decision details: see `TRADING_RULES.md`.
 127: 
 128: ## Execution
 129: 
 130: - `SignalProcessingStore`: `signal_processing_work` row created in the same
 131:   transaction as the `trade_signal`. Claim (REQUIRES_NEW, 3s, FOR UPDATE)
 132:   sets RUNNING + owner token. States: PENDING, RUNNING, COMPLETED, EXPIRED,
 133:   RETRYABLE_FAILURE, SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY (1/3/10s backoff), LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED,
 134:   REVIEW_REQUIRED (never auto-replayed). RUNNING > 5 min is quarantined.
 135: - `SignalProcessingCoordinator.process` business transaction: symbol lock
 136:   (`wallet_symbol_coordination`) -> ownership recheck -> position lock ->
 137:   lineage recheck -> `SignalProcessingFreshness` (after locks, DB clock with
 138:   exchange correction) -> `PaperTradingService.processSignalBody` ->
 139:   `store.complete`.
 140: - `SignalProcessingRecovery` (every 30s) resumes due WORKER/RECOVERY rows.
 141: - `WalletAutoExecutionService`: lock order symbol -> position ->
 142:   `wallet_mutation_coordination` -> daily stats. Idempotent by unique
 143:   `wallet_trade.execution_key`. `WalletMutationRepositoryGuard` rejects wallet
 144:   writes without the mutation lock.
 145: 
 146: ## Price evidence (`market_price_event`)
 147: 
 148: 1m direct observations: `recordPending` (INPUT tx; rejects >2s future, >15s
 149: stale vs exchange-corrected JVM clock; duplicate timestamp -> NON_CANONICAL)
 150: -> `finish` in PROTECTION tx -> APPLIED or NON_CANONICAL.
 151: `resolvePending` (every 5s, OFF) marks PENDING > 120s and not in flight as
 152: PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED (never replayed). Replay and execution price authority
 153: use only LEGACY/APPLIED rows. `ExchangeClockSynchronizer` measures Binance
 154: time vs DB and JVM (dedicated 5s client, no HTTP under locks).
 155: 
 156: ## Other components
 157: 
 158: | Area | Key classes | Notes |
 159: |---|---|---|
 160: | Order book | `OrderBookLiquidityService`, `OrderBookSnapshotService` | REST depth every 5s; best-effort async persistence, drops on overflow. |
 161: | Replay | `RegressionTestWorker`, `ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ReplayParityValidator` | Same production policy beans; simulated wallet; exact parity only with stored price events (`EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY`), else `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK`. Writes only `*_test` / archive tables. |
 162: | Inspector / activity | `TradeInspectorService`, `TradeActivityService` | Read-only except review marks / copy-to-proven. |
 163: | Health | `SystemHealthDailyService`, local-candle health | OFF-mode must not query retired shared tables. |
 164: | Monitor | `PriceMoveMonitorService`, `PriceMoveFinalizationWorker` | "Catching Market" retrospective blame. |
 165: | Audit | `ProductionExitAuditService` | Records real close trigger. |
 166: | Drain | `LocalDrainService` | ADMIN-only; stops admission, exits JVM on quiesce, resumes on timeout. |
 167: | Security | `SecurityConfig` | Form login, `app_user`; `/api/local/drain`, `/api/fix142/**` ADMIN. |
 168: | Whale | `whale/*` | Optional schedulers when `whale.enabled`. |
 169: | Approval console | `com.fix132.approval` | Separate app outside `com.crypto` scan; shared-market cutover only. |
 170: 
 171: ## Deployment
 172: 
 173: Jenkins: JDK 21 `mvn clean package` -> `Deploy-Trader.ps1` (mutex, single
 174: FIX-153 jar, SHA256, refuses unknown port-8080 owner, requires graceful
 175: `-DrainCommand`, never force-kills, verifies child PID and OFF guard). No
 176: automatic rollback. No deployment or production change without explicit user
 177: approval.

   6:

  11: @Component

  16: 
  17:     public BinanceStreamUrlBuilder(
  18:             BinanceMarketDataProperties properties,
  19:             CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService
  20:     ) {
  21:         this.properties = properties;
  22:         this.coinConfigurationService = coinConfigurationService;

  24: 
  25:     public String build() {
  26:         List<String> symbols = coinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols();
  27:         if (symbols.isEmpty()) {
  28:             throw new IllegalStateException("No enabled Binance symbols configured in Administration.");
  29:         }
  30:         if (properties.getIntervals().isEmpty()) {
  31:             throw new IllegalStateException("No Binance intervals configured.");
  32:         }
  33: 
  34:         String streams = symbols.stream()
  35:                 .flatMap(symbol -> properties.getIntervals().stream()
  36:                         .map(interval -> symbol.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
  37:                                 + "@kline_" + interval.trim()))
  38:                 .collect(Collectors.joining("/"));
  39: 
  40:         return properties.getWebsocketBaseUrl() + "/stream?streams=" + streams;
  41:     }
  42: }
  95:                 }else log.debug("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_NOT_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, proposedOutcome={}, transactionCompletion={}; durable outcome must be read",id,symbol,state,status);
  96:             }
  97:         });
  98:     }
  99:     /** Startup and periodic conditional resolution: no replay and no symbol-entry fence.
 100:      * The conditional update waits behind an in-flight effect TX and cannot override APPLIED.
 101:      * Runtime absence alone is insufficient; candidates must also exceed a configured bound. */
 102:     @org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-initial-delay-ms:5000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-delay-ms:5000}")
 103:     public void resolvePending(){

 106:             // FIX-153: housekeeping uses its own statement timeout, without mutating the shared template.
 107:             var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);
 108:             long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
 109:             var rows=bounded.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,observed_at FROM market_price_event WHERE source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING' AND created_at<? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 100",DIRECT_SOURCE,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(120)));

 111:                 int n=bounded.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status='PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED',outcome_reason='AUTOMATIC_ATOMIC_NO_COMMIT' WHERE id=? AND source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",id,DIRECT_SOURCE);
 112:                 if(n==1){com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_PENDING_RESOLVED);
 113:                     log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLVED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, outcome=PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED, evidence=ATOMIC_EFFECTS_AND_STATUS_NO_COMMIT, replay=false",id,row.get("symbol"),row.get("observed_at"));}
 114:             }
 115:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){log.warn("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLUTION_FAILED] durable outcome unchanged; no live fence introduced",failure);}

codex
## FINDINGS

1. Default checked-in mode is `OFF`; in that mode the trader owns Binance WebSocket and REST candle ingestion and persists locally to `crypto_ai.candle`. `LIVE` disables those direct paths and reads candles through the configured collector datasource. No production environment was accessed.

2. `OBSERVE` is materially unsafe against the pending direction: direct WebSocket/REST ingestion is disabled only for `LIVE`, while `SharedMarketConsumer` starts its collector polling/dispatch clocks in every non-`OFF` mode. Therefore OBSERVE can operate both paths concurrently.

3. Runtime candle persistence has a real monotonic close mechanism. A runtime close gets a token only on an atomic `closed=0 → 1` update; later open updates and duplicate closes cannot overwrite the completed candle or generate another dispatch token. The close event is published only after input, protection, and observer transactions.

4. Ordinary REST bootstrap/backfill violates the pending recovery contract. Its `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` can finalize an existing open runtime candle, but preserves the existing provenance/token and creates neither reconciliation audit nor a `candle_analysis_outcome`. It is therefore not “insert missing identities only,” despite the source comment.

5. The reported coin-activation race is technically credible. Activation reloads the all-symbol combined WebSocket before running bootstrap. If bootstrap finalizes a just-closed, already-open runtime candle before its final WebSocket close message is processed, that later close cannot obtain a token or dispatch analysis. The detector excludes it because it has no token; reconciliation excludes it because it is no longer open. I found no test covering this interleaving.

6. A specialized reconciliation slice exists and is materially safer: it uses update-only recovery for an existing open row and insert-if-absent for a wholly absent candle, records audit/outcome evidence, and never dispatches recovered work. But it is disabled by the checked-in configuration, so it is not the current active default behavior.

7. Reconciliation still has unproven/material weaknesses even if enabled:
   - It takes `LIMIT 50` per interval before due-status and loss-evidence filtering.
   - It does not restrict open candidates to enabled symbols.
   - Reconnect loss evidence is process-local (`lastReconnectAdmission`) and disappears across restart.
   - Retryable candidates age out of the quality window without a demonstrated terminal age-out outcome.
   - REST clients have bounded timeouts but no shared 429/418, `Retry-After`, ban, or global request-budget coordination.

8. The detector addresses the commit-to-dispatch crash window only while a tokened candle remains inside its bounded lookback. Retry state is in memory; after a sufficiently long outage, an old unanalysed tokened candle falls outside SQL selection without a detector-created terminal outcome. This is confirmed by code and even directly reflected in the unit test that asserts old runtime candles are not analysed.

9. Replay reads closed local/selected-source candles by symbol, interval, time, and `closed=true`. It does not classify provenance, close token, reconciliation audit, outcome, or “historical-only” recovery state. It provides narrower price-event checks, not demonstrated parity for candle recovery/admission behavior.

10. Documentation has material drift:
    - AGENTS.md and `codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` call V103 an unconditional fresh trader-only blocker, but `B105` expressly establishes a trader-only baseline intended to bypass V103; a source-level resolution test exists. A real disposable-MySQL success report was not found, so this corrects the categorical claim without proving deployment readiness.
    - `claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` incorrectly groups OBSERVE with LIVE/shared-only operation; code allows direct ingestion in OBSERVE.
    - `MarketDataBootstrapService` and `CandleRepository` comments claim missing-only backfill, contradicted by their implementation.

## OBJECTIONS

- Default OFF-mode bootstrap can silently consume a live close without analysis/outcome evidence.
- Default reconciliation is disabled; enabled reconciliation still lacks demonstrated fair candidate selection, durable restart evidence, terminal ageing, and coordinated Binance rate-limit handling.
- OBSERVE permits direct and shared-market ingestion together, contrary to the pending architecture.
- Replay parity does not cover the implemented recovery/admission classifications.
- Required MySQL, crash/restart, concurrency, replay, and deployment-replacement proof is absent.

Candidate for later solution review only: separate ordinary historical import from authority to finalize an existing runtime-open row, then test the activation/reconnect boundary against disposable MySQL.

## UNKNOWNS

- Production artifact, overrides, mode, database/Flyway history, collector availability, and actual operational behavior were intentionally not accessed.
- Whether the activation race has occurred in reality.
- Whether `LocalCandleMySqlIT` has passed on disposable MySQL; the source test exists, but no saved report was found.
- Whether every administrative coin mutation invokes stream reload; the documented reload paths do, but no runtime audit was inspected.
- Open-position protection coverage during application replacement.
